- **jira-cloud-rest-api-forbidden-403-erro-f56014ea05e182cb**: JIRA Cloud REST API : Forbidden (403) error. The solution in the end was to use Basic Authentication via the Authorization header when issuing requests to JIRA's Cloud REST API: https://CLOUD_ID.atlassian.net/rest/api/3/API_METHOD Headers: 'Authorization': 'Basic ZGFjcmVAb...', 'Accept': 'application/json' Basic authentication will be removed in the future according to the API documentation , so this is being treated as a stop-gap solution. Use when hitting this exact issue with JIRA Cloud REST ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jira-cloud-rest-api-forbidden-403-erro-f56014ea05e182cb))
